FILE: README.md
    1: # Tuần 1 — Movie dataset và pipeline log Apache Flume
    2: 
    3: **Người phụ trách: Nhan Tử Mạnh.** Đề tài: “Phân tích dữ liệu phim và log hành vi người dùng trên hệ thống xem phim”.
    4: 
    5: **Trạng thái nghiệm thu Tuần 1 (cập nhật 04/10/2026):** Đã hoàn thành 100% mục tiêu Tuần 1. Apache Flume là công nghệ trọng tâm của đồ án được triển khai thành công trên Docker. Dữ liệu phim MovieLens đã chuẩn hóa (9.742 phim). Bộ sinh log hành vi người dùng hoạt động chính xác (10.000 demo events và 1.000.000 main events). Pipeline Flume (`spooldir` Source → `file` Channel → `file_roll` Sink) đã build và chạy thực tế trên Docker thành công: demo 10.000 events đạt PASS E2E (`evidence/run-a9b3a8a26afb/`), main 1.000.000 events đạt PASS E2E (`evidence/run-f63c810e9d8d/`). Kết quả reconciliation chứng minh 0 missing, 0 duplicates, 0 payload changed. Toàn bộ 12 unit tests Python PASS. Sẵn sàng bàn giao cho Thành viên 2 xác thực downstream.
    6: 
    7: ## 1. Mục tiêu và kiến trúc
    8: 
    9: Chuẩn bị metadata phim và log hành vi mô phỏng phục vụ học tập, thu thập qua Flume rồi bàn giao JSONL cho nhóm PySpark downstream. Apache Flume đóng vai trò công nghệ trung tâm trong việc thu thập và vận chuyển log. Không triển khai website, đăng nhập, dashboard hay phân tích sâu tuần 2.
   10: 
   11: ```text
   12: MovieLens ZIP → raw CSV → chuẩn hóa → processed/movies.csv
   13:                                       ↓ chọn movieId hợp lệ
   14: generate_logs.py → generated/demo.jsonl (đóng file hoàn toàn)
   15:  → copy .tmp → rename nguyên tử .jsonl trong spool/
   16:  → Spooling Directory Source → File Channel → File Roll Sink
   17:  → filesystem cục bộ: data/runs/<run-id>/output/*.jsonl
   18: ```
   19: 
   20: Đây là filesystem cục bộ được bind mount từ container, **không phải HDFS**. Không có yêu cầu bắt buộc HDFS trong đầu bài nên chọn cách triển khai đơn giản. Một triệu event mô phỏng không tự chứng minh hệ thống đạt quy mô Big Data.
   21: 
   22: ## 2. Môi trường và phiên bản
   23: 
   24: | Thành phần | Lựa chọn | Kiểm chứng hiện tại |
   25: |---|---|---|
   26: | Python | >=3.10, thư viện chuẩn, không cần pip | Đã chạy bằng Python 3.11/3.12 đi kèm môi trường |
   27: | Apache Flume | 1.11.0, binary chính thức, kiểm SHA512 lúc build | Đã build image và chạy thành công trên Docker (`movie-log-flume:1.11.0-local`) |
   28: | Java trong container | Eclipse Temurin 8 JRE, `eclipse-temurin:8-jre-jammy` | Đã kiểm chứng runtime: OpenJDK 1.8.0_504 trong container Flume |
   29: | Docker | Docker Engine + Compose v2, Linux containers | Đã kiểm chứng: Docker Engine 29.8.1 + Compose v5.5.1 hoạt động tốt |
   30: | Hệ điều hành khảo sát | Windows 10 build 19045 / Windows 11 | `evidence/environment.json` |
   31: 
   32: Chọn Docker để cố định đường dẫn Linux và tránh cài Flume trực tiếp trên Windows. Docker Desktop cung cấp môi trường Linux containers ổn định cho pipeline Flume. Xem [yêu cầu cài Docker Desktop chính thức](https://docs.docker.com/desktop/setup/install/windows-install/).
   33: 
   34: Flume yêu cầu Java từ 8 theo [User Guide](https://flume.apache.org/FlumeUserGuide). Nguồn binary ở [Apache Downloads](https://flume.apache.org/download.html); base image ở [Docker Official Images](https://hub.docker.com/_/eclipse-temurin). Runner lưu image ID sau build để truy vết.
   35: 
   36: ## 3. Cài đặt và thư mục chạy lệnh
   37: 
   38: Giải nén/chuyển cả thư mục `movie-log-bigdata` sang vị trí làm bài. **Mọi lệnh dưới đây chạy tại thư mục gốc `movie-log-bigdata`.** Mã không dùng gói Python bên ngoài; `requirements.txt` cố ý chỉ có chú thích.
   39: 
   40: **PowerShell trên Windows:** cài Python >=3.10 và Docker Desktop theo tài liệu chính thức, chọn Linux containers, mở Docker Desktop. Chạy:
   41: 
   42: ```powershell
   43: cd C:\duong-dan\movie-log-bigdata
   44: python --version
   45: docker version
   46: docker compose version
   47: python scripts/check_environment.py
   48: ```
   49: 
   50: `C:\duong-dan` là vị trí bạn đặt dự án. Nếu `python` là Windows App execution alias, dùng `py -3` hoặc đường dẫn tuyệt đối tới Python thật. Không cần cài Java trên host vì Java nằm trong image.
   51: 
   52: **Terminal Linux:** vào thư mục đã chép dự án, dùng `python3` thay `python`; kiểm tra Docker/Compose như trên. Không chạy `run_flume.sh` trực tiếp trên host: script này được Docker entrypoint gọi với đường dẫn trong container.
   53: 
   54: Gợi ý tài nguyên cho đồ án: RAM máy 8 GB trở lên, cấp khoảng 2 GB cho Docker, chừa ít nhất 5 GB đĩa. Đây là ngân sách dự kiến, chưa phải benchmark. JVM cấu hình 512 MB heap; file channel cũng dùng đĩa và bộ nhớ ngoài heap.
   55: 
   56: ## 4. Dataset: tải và chuẩn hóa
   57: 
   58: Bản bàn giao đã có dữ liệu. Có thể kiểm lại snapshot mà không tải lại:
   59: 
   60: ```powershell
   61: python scripts/download_dataset.py
   62: ```
   63: 
   64: Nếu chạy từ bản mã nguồn Git sạch chưa có data:
   65: 
   66: ```powershell
   67: python scripts/download_dataset.py
   68: python scripts/prepare_movies.py
   69: ```
   70: 
   71: Downloader giữ ZIP, README điều kiện sử dụng và CSV nguyên bản; ghi thời gian UTC, hash SHA256. Snapshot hiện có chỉ được tái sử dụng khi hash còn đúng. `prepare_movies.py` không ghi đè output đang có. Muốn chuẩn hóa lại để so sánh:
   72: 
   73: ```powershell
   74: python scripts/prepare_movies.py --output data/processed/movies-check.csv --report evidence/dataset-quality-check.json
   75: ```
   76: 
   77: Muốn tải snapshot khác: `--raw data/raw-new`, rồi truyền `--source data/raw-new/ml-latest-small/movies.csv` và một `--output` mới. Không thay raw cũ.
   78: 
   79: Nếu lỗi chứng thư TLS, cấu hình CA tin cậy của máy hoặc dùng `--ca-file C:\duong-dan\trusted-ca.pem`; không tắt kiểm chứng HTTPS. Trong phiên bàn giao, đã dùng CA gốc từ runtime Node để Python tải HTTPS có kiểm chứng; chi tiết ở `evidence/execution_commands.md`.
   80: 
   81: ## 5. Sinh và kiểm tra log demo
   82: 
   83: Demo đã có sẵn `data/generated/demo.jsonl`. Lệnh tái tạo tại đường dẫn mới:
   84: 
   85: ```powershell
   86: python scripts/generate_logs.py --mode demo --output data/generated/demo-repeat.jsonl
   87: python scripts/validate_pipeline.py --input data/generated/demo-repeat.jsonl --input-only --report evidence/demo-repeat-validation.json
   88: python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
   89: ```
   90: 
   91: Trên bản Git sạch dùng `python scripts/generate_logs.py --mode demo` để tạo đường dẫn mặc định. Cùng seed, tham số và dataset tạo cùng nội dung/eventId. Chỉ dùng `--run-id` mới nếu muốn mô phỏng một lô độc lập; không đổi ID khi thử lại cùng một lô thất bại.
   92: 
   93: Có thể đổi `--events`, `--users`, `--start`, `--end`, `--seed`, `--output`. Timestamp bắt buộc có timezone; khoảng thời gian ít nhất 6 giờ. Mặc định mô phỏng tháng 09/2026 giờ Việt Nam, xuất UTC `Z`.
   94: 
   95: ## 6. Chạy Flume và thu bằng chứng E2E
   96: 
   97: **PowerShell hoặc Terminal Linux (đổi `python` thành `python3`):**
   98: 
   99: ```powershell
  100: python scripts/run_pipeline.py --input data/generated/demo.jsonl --timeout 300
  101: ```
  102: 
  103: Runner kiểm input, build image, bật agent, đưa file đã ghi xong vào spool, chờ `.COMPLETED` và lượng output, dừng agent để đóng file, rồi đối chiếu payload/eventId. Mỗi lần chạy tạo `data/runs/run-...`, `logs/run-...`, `evidence/run-...` riêng; không xóa lần chạy trước. Có thể tăng `--timeout 900` nếu máy chậm. Thời gian build không tính trong timeout chờ dữ liệu.
  104: 
  105: Chỉ khi `evidence/<run-id>/status.json` và `reconciliation.json` cùng ghi `PASS` mới coi demo E2E đạt. Xem thêm log vận hành để xác nhận agent không lỗi cấu hình. `INPUT_PASS` chỉ xác nhận dữ liệu trước Flume.
  106: 
  107: Runner lưu: lệnh + cwd + biến mount, phiên bản Docker/Java/Flume, build log, resolved Compose, image ID, stdout/stderr container có timestamp, mẫu input/output và báo cáo đối chiếu. Sau chạy, container được dừng và gỡ; thư mục dữ liệu/channel/log/evidence giữ lại.
  108: 
  109: Nếu cần kiểm lại output, thay `run-...` bằng mã thực tế runner in ra:
  110: 
  111: ```powershell
  112: python scripts/validate_pipeline.py --input data/generated/demo.jsonl --output-dir data/runs/run-.../output --report evidence/recheck-demo.json
  113: ```
  114: 
  115: Không trộn output nhiều lần chạy khi đối chiếu một input. Source hoàn tất không có nghĩa sink đã xả hết channel; vì vậy phải chờ/dừng có kiểm soát rồi kiểm tra.
  116: 
  117: ## 7. Dữ liệu chính — chỉ sau khi demo E2E đạt
  118: 
  119: ```powershell
  120: python scripts/generate_logs.py --mode main --events 1000000 --users 1000 --start 2026-09-01T00:00:00+07:00 --end 2026-10-01T00:00:00+07:00 --seed 42 --output data/generated/main.jsonl
  121: python scripts/validate_pipeline.py --input data/generated/main.jsonl --input-only --report evidence/main-input-validation.json
  122: python scripts/run_pipeline.py --input data/generated/main.jsonl --timeout 1800 --demo-proof evidence/run-DEMO-THUC-TE/status.json
  123: ```
  124: 
  125: Thay `run-DEMO-THUC-TE` bằng lần demo PASS thật (ví dụ `evidence/run-a9b3a8a26afb/status.json`). Thực tế lô main 1.000.000 events đã được thực thi và xác thực PASS tại `evidence/run-f63c810e9d8d/status.json`. Runner từ chối input >10.000 event nếu thiếu chứng cứ demo hợp lệ với cùng hash cấu hình. Một triệu event chiếm ~343 MB JSONL, sinh ra 254 file output qua File Roll Sink. Đổi `--events 100000` nếu tài nguyên ít; tăng số user/khoảng thời gian nếu mô phỏng quá dày. Không dùng seed khác để che lỗi kiểm thử.
  126: 
  127: ## 8. Vị trí sản phẩm và xử lý lỗi
  128: 
  129: | Vị trí | Nội dung |
  130: |---|---|
  131: | `data/raw/` | ZIP, CSV gốc, giấy phép và manifest |
  132: | `data/processed/movies.csv` | Metadata chuẩn hóa, thêm releaseYear |
  133: | `data/generated/` | Input JSONL và manifest; không phải sink output |
  134: | `data/runs/<run-id>/spool/` | File đã nạp, `.COMPLETED`, tracker |
  135: | `data/runs/<run-id>/channel/` | File Channel, không phải dữ liệu cho Spark |
  136: | `data/runs/<run-id>/output/` | JSONL thực tế do sink tạo |
  137: | `logs/<run-id>/` và `evidence/<run-id>/flume-operational.log` | Log vận hành, tách khỏi event |
  138: | `evidence/` | Kiểm tra môi trường, QA dataset/input, kiểm thử và lần chạy |
  139: | `docs/` | Mô tả dữ liệu, schema, pipeline, báo cáo, thuyết trình, bàn giao |
  140: 
  141: | Lỗi | Cách xử lý |
  142: |---|---|
  143: | Docker not found / cannot connect | Cài/mở Docker, kiểm `docker version` phải thấy Server; xác nhận Linux containers |
  144: | WSL E_ACCESSDENIED / virtualization | Kiểm tra quyền và khả năng ảo hóa với người quản trị; có thể chuyển sang máy Linux có Docker |
  145: | Build tải Flume/CA thất bại | Xem build.log, kiểm HTTPS/CA/proxy; không bỏ kiểm hash |
  146: | FileExistsError | Chọn output mới; không sửa file đã vào spool |
  147: | Source không nhận file | Kiểm tên `.jsonl`, quyền mount, agent log và regex; `.tmp` cố ý bị bỏ qua |
  148: | File channel hết đĩa/khóa | Chừa trên 100 MB, không cho hai agent dùng chung channel; không xóa checkpoint khi còn dữ liệu |
  149: | Timeout/output thiếu | Xem log, capacity/quyền đĩa; giữ run dir để điều tra; chạy lại runner tạo vùng mới |
  150: | Event trùng | Xem số duplicates và payload conflicts; khử trùng eventId ở Spark sau khi kiểm xung đột |
  151: | JSON bị tách dòng | Không đưa JSON pretty-print nhiều dòng vào spool; event phải dưới giới hạn 16.384 ký tự |
  152: | Mô phỏng quá dày | Tăng `--users` hoặc thời gian; file `.part` không được publish |
  153: 
  154: Chi tiết: [dataset](docs/dataset_description.md), [schema](docs/log_schema.md), [pipeline](docs/flume_pipeline.md), [báo cáo tuần 1](docs/week1_report.md), [thuyết trình](docs/presentation_script.md), [bàn giao PySpark](docs/week2_handoff.md).

FILE: requirements.txt
    1: # Python >=3.10; standard library only. No pip dependencies.

FILE: scripts/check_environment.py
    1: """Record actual environment and tool exit codes without installing software."""
    2: import json
    3: import platform
    4: import shutil
    5: import subprocess
    6: import sys
    7: from common import utcnow, write_json
    8: 
    9: result = {'checked_at_utc': utcnow(), 'os': platform.platform(), 'python': sys.version,
   10:           'python_executable': sys.executable, 'tools': {}}
   11: for args in [['java', '-version'], ['docker', 'version'], ['docker', 'compose', 'version'], ['wsl', '--status'], ['git', '--version']]:
   12:     key = ' '.join(args)
   13:     if not shutil.which(args[0]):
   14:         result['tools'][key] = {'available': False, 'reason': 'not found in PATH'}
   15:         continue
   16:     try:
   17:         proc = subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, timeout=20)
   18:         content = proc.stdout + proc.stderr
   19:         encoding = 'utf-16-le' if b'\x00' in content else 'utf-8'
   20:         result['tools'][key] = {'available': proc.returncode == 0, 'exit_code': proc.returncode,
   21:                                'output': content.decode(encoding, errors='replace')}
   22:     except Exception as exc:
   23:         result['tools'][key] = {'available': False, 'reason': str(exc)}
   24: write_json('evidence/environment.json', result)
   25: print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

FILE: scripts/common.py
    1: """Shared standard-library helpers; run CLI scripts from the project root."""
    2: import hashlib
    3: import json
    4: from datetime import datetime, timezone
    5: from pathlib import Path
    6: 
    7: 
    8: def sha256(path):
    9:     h = hashlib.sha256()
   10:     with open(path, 'rb') as f:
   11:         for block in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
   12:             h.update(block)
   13:     return h.hexdigest()
   14: 
   15: 
   16: def write_json(path, obj):
   17:     path = Path(path)
   18:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   19:     path.write_text(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
   20: 
   21: 
   22: def utcnow():
   23:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
   24: 
   25: 
   26: def parse_time(value):
   27:     dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   28:     if dt.tzinfo is None:
   29:         raise ValueError('Timestamp must include timezone, e.g. +07:00 or Z')
   30:     return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)

FILE: scripts/download_dataset.py
    1: """Download immutable local snapshot; preserve source ZIP and license README."""
    2: import argparse
    3: import json
    4: import shutil
    5: import ssl
    6: import urllib.request
    7: import zipfile
    8: from pathlib import Path
    9: from common import sha256, utcnow, write_json
   10: 
   11: URL = 'https://files.grouplens.org/datasets/movielens/ml-latest-small.zip'
   12: 
   13: 
   14: def download(raw, ca_file=None):
   15:     raw = Path(raw)
   16:     raw.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   17:     archive = raw / 'ml-latest-small.zip'
   18:     manifest = raw / 'download_manifest.json'
   19:     if manifest.exists():
   20:         saved = json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
   21:         if not archive.exists() or sha256(archive) != saved['sha256']:
   22:             raise ValueError('Existing snapshot checksum mismatch; use a NEW raw directory')
   23:         for name, digest in saved['files_sha256'].items():
   24:             if sha256(raw / name) != digest:
   25:                 raise ValueError('Raw file modified: ' + name)
   26:         print(json.dumps(saved, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
   27:         return saved
   28:     if archive.exists() or (raw / 'ml-latest-small').exists():
   29:         raise FileExistsError('Incomplete/untracked raw snapshot; choose a new --raw directory')
   30:     part = raw / 'download.zip.part'
   31:     if part.exists():
   32:         raise FileExistsError(part)
   33:     context = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=ca_file)
   34:     with urllib.request.urlopen(URL, context=context, timeout=120) as response, part.open('xb') as out:
   35:         shutil.copyfileobj(response, out)
   36:     with zipfile.ZipFile(part) as z:
   37:         if z.testzip() is not None:
   38:             raise ValueError('ZIP CRC check failed')
   39:         expected = {f'ml-latest-small/{x}' for x in ['README.txt', 'movies.csv', 'ratings.csv', 'tags.csv', 'links.csv']}
   40:         if not expected.issubset(z.namelist()):
   41:             raise ValueError('Unexpected ZIP contents')
   42:         # Extract ONLY explicit files: never trust archive path traversal.
   43:         for name in sorted(expected):
   44:             target = raw / name
   45:             target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   46:             with z.open(name) as source, target.open('xb') as out:
   47:                 shutil.copyfileobj(source, out)
   48:     part.rename(archive)
   49:     result = {'dataset': 'MovieLens latest-small', 'source_url': 'https://grouplens.org/datasets/movielens/latest/',
   50:               'download_url': URL, 'downloaded_at_utc': utcnow(), 'tls_verified': True,
   51:               'sha256': sha256(archive), 'archive_bytes': archive.stat().st_size,
   52:               'license_file': 'ml-latest-small/README.txt',
   53:               'files_sha256': {n: sha256(raw / n) for n in sorted(expected)}}
   54:     write_json(manifest, result)
   55:     print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
   56:     return result
   57: 
   58: 
   59: if __name__ == '__main__':
   60:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   61:     p.add_argument('--raw', default='data/raw')
   62:     p.add_argument('--ca-file', help='Optional trusted PEM CA bundle; TLS verification stays enabled')
   63:     a = p.parse_args()
   64:     download(a.raw, a.ca_file)

FILE: scripts/generate_logs.py
    1: """Stream complete, coherent sessions. Synthetic data, not real behavior."""
    2: import argparse
    3: import bisect
    4: import csv
    5: import hashlib
    6: import itertools
    7: import json
    8: import random
    9: import uuid
   10: from collections import Counter
   11: from datetime import timedelta, timezone
   12: from pathlib import Path
   13: from common import parse_time, sha256, write_json
   14: 
   15: LOCAL = timezone(timedelta(hours=7))
   16: 
   17: 
   18: def cdf(weights):
   19:     return list(itertools.accumulate(weights))
   20: 
   21: 
   22: def pick(rng, cumulative):
   23:     return bisect.bisect_right(cumulative, rng.random() * cumulative[-1])
   24: 
   25: 
   26: def generate(movies, output, events=1000000, users=1000, start='2026-09-01T00:00:00+07:00',
   27:              end='2026-10-01T00:00:00+07:00', seed=42, run_id=None):
   28:     output = Path(output)
   29:     start_dt, end_dt = parse_time(start), parse_time(end)
   30:     if events < 1 or users < 1 or end_dt - start_dt < timedelta(hours=6):
   31:         raise ValueError('events/users must be positive and time range must be at least 6 hours')
   32:     if output.exists() or output.with_suffix(output.suffix + '.manifest.json').exists():
   33:         raise FileExistsError(output)
   34:     with open(movies, encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:
   35:         ids = [int(r['movieId']) for r in csv.DictReader(f)]
   36:     if not ids or len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
   37:         raise ValueError('Movie IDs must be nonempty and unique')
   38:     identity = {'movies_sha256': sha256(movies), 'events': events, 'users': users,
   39:                 'start': start_dt.isoformat(), 'end': end_dt.isoformat(), 'seed': seed,
   40:                 'generator_version': 1}
   41:     run_id = run_id or hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:20]
   42:     namespace = uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_URL, 'movie-log-bigdata:' + run_id)
   43:     rng = random.Random(seed)
   44:     rng.shuffle(ids)  # Popularity is synthetic, independent of ID/year and original ratings.
   45:     movie_cdf = cdf([1 / (rank + 1) ** 0.85 for rank in range(len(ids))])
   46:     user_order = list(range(1, users + 1))
   47:     rng.shuffle(user_order)
   48:     user_cdf = cdf([1 / (rank + 1) ** 0.65 for rank in range(users)])
   49:     runtime = {mid: 3600 + (int(hashlib.sha256(f'{seed}:{mid}'.encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16) % 121) * 60 for mid in ids}
   50:     latest_start = end_dt - timedelta(hours=4)
   51:     hours, weights = [], []
   52:     cursor = start_dt.replace(minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
   53:     while cursor <= latest_start:
   54:         lo, hi = max(cursor, start_dt), min(cursor + timedelta(hours=1), latest_start)
   55:         seconds = int((hi - lo).total_seconds())
   56:         if seconds > 0:
   57:             hour = cursor.astimezone(LOCAL).hour
   58:             hours.append((lo, seconds))
   59:             weights.append(seconds * (5 if 19 <= hour <= 23 else 0.5 if hour < 6 else 1.5))
   60:         cursor += timedelta(hours=1)
   61:     hour_cdf = cdf(weights)
   62:     # Store occupied intervals per user; reject overlaps instead of inventing concurrent viewing.
   63:     busy = {u: [] for u in user_order}
   64:     counters, hour_counts, user_counts, movie_counts = Counter(), Counter(), Counter(), Counter()
   65:     output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   66:     partial = output.with_suffix(output.suffix + '.part')
   67:     written = sessions = 0
   68:     with partial.open('x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as f:
   69:         while written < events:
   70:             left = events - written
   71:             mid = ids[pick(rng, movie_cdf)]
   72:             duration = runtime[mid]
   73:             if left < 3 or rng.random() < 0.15:
   74:                 actions = [('view', 0, 0)]
   75:             else:
   76:                 complete = rng.random() < 0.55
   77:                 watched = duration if complete else rng.randint(30, duration - 1)
   78:                 delay = rng.randint(2, 15)
   79:                 terminal = 'complete' if complete else 'stop'
   80:                 if left >= 5 and rng.random() < 0.35:
   81:                     first = rng.randint(1, watched - 1)
   82:                     pause = rng.randint(10, 300)
   83:                     actions = [('view', 0, 0), ('play', delay, 0), ('pause', delay + first, first),
   84:                                ('play', delay + first + pause, 0), (terminal, delay + watched + pause, watched - first)]
   85:                 else:
   86:                     actions = [('view', 0, 0), ('play', delay, 0), (terminal, delay + watched, watched)]
   87:             span = actions[-1][1] + 1
   88:             for attempt in range(5000):
   89:                 uid = user_order[pick(rng, user_cdf)]
   90:                 lo, seconds = hours[pick(rng, hour_cdf)]
   91:                 when = lo + timedelta(seconds=rng.randrange(seconds))
   92:                 until = when + timedelta(seconds=span)
   93:                 slots = busy[uid]
   94:                 pos = bisect.bisect_left(slots, (when, when))
   95:                 if (pos == 0 or slots[pos - 1][1] <= when) and (pos == len(slots) or until <= slots[pos][0]):
   96:                     slots.insert(pos, (when, until))
   97:                     break
   98:             else:
   99:                 raise ValueError('Time range/user population too crowded; increase --users or time range. Partial file not published.')
  100:             sessions += 1
  101:             sid = str(uuid.uuid5(namespace, f'session:{sessions}'))
  102:             user_counts[str(uid)] += 1
  103:             movie_counts[str(mid)] += 1
  104:             hour_counts[str(when.astimezone(LOCAL).hour)] += 1
  105:             for seq, (action, offset, delta) in enumerate(actions):
  106:                 event = {'eventId': str(uuid.uuid5(namespace, f'{sessions}:{seq}')), 'userId': f'sim-user-{uid:06d}',
  107:                          'sessionId': sid, 'movieId': mid, 'action': action,
  108:                          'timestamp': (when + timedelta(seconds=offset)).isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z'),
  109:                          'watchDuration': delta, 'sequenceNumber': seq, 'runId': run_id,
  110:                          'isSynthetic': True, 'simulatedMovieDuration': duration, 'durationSource': 'synthetic'}
  111:                 f.write(json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) + '\n')
  112:                 written += 1
  113:                 counters[action] += 1
  114:     partial.rename(output)
  115:     result = {**identity, 'runId': run_id, 'output': str(output), 'sha256': sha256(output),
  116:               'bytes': output.stat().st_size, 'sessions': sessions, 'actions': dict(counters),
  117:               'session_start_hour_Asia_Saigon': dict(sorted(hour_counts.items(), key=lambda x: int(x[0]))),
  118:               'active_users': len(user_counts), 'unique_movies': len(movie_counts),
  119:               'top_users_sessions': user_counts.most_common(10), 'top_movies_sessions': movie_counts.most_common(10),
  120:               'status': 'GENERATED_NOT_FLUME_OUTPUT'}
  121:     write_json(output.with_suffix(output.suffix + '.manifest.json'), result)
  122:     print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
  123:     return result
  124: 
  125: 
  126: if __name__ == '__main__':
  127:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  128:     p.add_argument('--movies', default='data/processed/movies.csv')
  129:     p.add_argument('--mode', choices=['demo', 'main'], default='main')
  130:     p.add_argument('--events', type=int)
  131:     p.add_argument('--users', type=int, default=1000)
  132:     p.add_argument('--start', default='2026-09-01T00:00:00+07:00')
  133:     p.add_argument('--end', default='2026-10-01T00:00:00+07:00')
  134:     p.add_argument('--seed', type=int, default=42)
  135:     p.add_argument('--run-id', help='Use a new value for an independent run; same parameters default to reproducible IDs')
  136:     p.add_argument('--output')
  137:     a = p.parse_args()
  138:     generate(a.movies, a.output or f'data/generated/{a.mode}.jsonl',
  139:              a.events if a.events is not None else (10000 if a.mode == 'demo' else 1000000),
  140:              a.users, a.start, a.end, a.seed, a.run_id)

FILE: scripts/prepare_movies.py
    1: """Strict UTF-8 / CSV validation, separate normalized metadata and QA report."""
    2: import argparse
    3: import csv
    4: import json
    5: import re
    6: import unicodedata
    7: from collections import Counter
    8: from pathlib import Path
    9: from common import sha256, utcnow, write_json
   10: 
   11: 
   12: def prepare(source, output, report):
   13:     source, output = Path(source), Path(output)
   14:     if output.exists():
   15:         raise FileExistsError('Use a new --output; normalized data will not be overwritten')
   16:     counts = Counter()
   17:     rows, seen, issues = [], set(), []
   18:     with source.open(encoding='utf-8', errors='strict', newline='') as f:
   19:         reader = csv.DictReader(f, strict=True)
   20:         if reader.fieldnames != ['movieId', 'title', 'genres']:
   21:             raise ValueError('Unexpected CSV header')
   22:         for line, row in enumerate(reader, 2):
   23:             counts['input_rows'] += 1
   24:             if None in row or any(v is None for v in row.values()):
   25:                 raise ValueError(f'Malformed CSV row {line}')
   26:             if any('\ufffd' in v or '\x00' in v for v in row.values()):
   27:                 raise ValueError(f'Encoding replacement/NUL character on row {line}')
   28:             try:
   29:                 mid = int(row['movieId'])
   30:                 assert mid > 0
   31:             except (ValueError, AssertionError):
   32:                 raise ValueError(f'Invalid movieId on row {line}')
   33:             if mid in seen:
   34:                 counts['duplicate_movie_ids'] += 1
   35:                 issues.append({'line': line, 'movieId': mid, 'issue': 'duplicate_movieId'})
   36:             seen.add(mid)
   37:             title = unicodedata.normalize('NFC', row['title'].strip())
   38:             genres = '|'.join(dict.fromkeys(g.strip() for g in row['genres'].split('|') if g.strip()))
   39:             counts['missing_title'] += not bool(title)
   40:             counts['missing_genres'] += not bool(genres)
   41:             counts['no_genres_listed'] += genres == '(no genres listed)'
   42:             match = re.search(r'\((\d{4})\)\s*$', title)
   43:             year = int(match.group(1)) if match else None
   44:             # Fixed, documented range makes cleaning reproducible across dates.
   45:             if year is not None and not 1870 <= year <= 2100:
   46:                 year = None
   47:             if year is None:
   48:                 counts['missing_or_unparsed_release_year'] += 1
   49:                 issues.append({'line': line, 'movieId': mid, 'issue': 'releaseYear_unavailable', 'title': title})
   50:             counts['normalized_rows_changed'] += title != row['title'] or genres != row['genres'] or str(mid) != row['movieId']
   51:             rows.append({'movieId': mid, 'title': title, 'genres': genres, 'releaseYear': year if year is not None else ''})
   52:     stats = {k: counts[k] for k in ['input_rows', 'duplicate_movie_ids', 'missing_title', 'missing_genres', 'no_genres_listed', 'missing_or_unparsed_release_year', 'normalized_rows_changed']}
   53:     stats.update({'source': str(source), 'source_sha256': sha256(source), 'checked_at_utc': utcnow(),
   54:                   'utf8_decode_errors': 0, 'csv_format_errors': 0, 'issues': issues,
   55:                   'related_csv_rows': {}})
   56:     for name in ['ratings', 'tags', 'links']:
   57:         sibling = source.with_name(name + '.csv')
   58:         if sibling.exists():
   59:             with sibling.open(encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:
   60:                 stats['related_csv_rows'][name] = sum(1 for _ in csv.DictReader(f, strict=True))
   61:     if not rows or counts['duplicate_movie_ids'] or counts['missing_title']:
   62:         stats['status'] = 'FAIL'
   63:         write_json(report, stats)
   64:         raise ValueError('Fatal data quality errors; inspect QA report')
   65:     output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   66:     with output.open('x', encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:
   67:         writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=['movieId', 'title', 'genres', 'releaseYear'], lineterminator='\n')
   68:         writer.writeheader()
   69:         writer.writerows(rows)
   70:     stats.update({'status': 'PASS', 'output_rows': len(rows), 'output_sha256': sha256(output)})
   71:     write_json(report, stats)
   72:     print(json.dumps(stats, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
   73:     return stats
   74: 
   75: 
   76: if __name__ == '__main__':
   77:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   78:     p.add_argument('--source', default='data/raw/ml-latest-small/movies.csv')
   79:     p.add_argument('--output', default='data/processed/movies.csv')
   80:     p.add_argument('--report', default='evidence/dataset_quality.json')
   81:     a = p.parse_args()
   82:     prepare(a.source, a.output, a.report)

FILE: scripts/run_flume.sh
    1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
    2: set -euo pipefail
    3: mkdir -p /data/spool /data/output /data/channel/checkpoint /data/channel/data /logs
    4: export FLUME_LOG_DIR=/logs
    5: exec /opt/flume/bin/flume-ng agent \
    6:   --name a1 --conf /opt/flume/conf --conf-file /project/config/flume.conf \
    7:   -Dflume.root.logger=INFO,console

FILE: scripts/run_pipeline.py
    1: """Real Docker/Flume E2E run with isolated directories and saved evidence."""
    2: import argparse
    3: import json
    4: import os
    5: import shutil
    6: import subprocess
    7: import time
    8: import uuid
    9: from pathlib import Path
   10: from common import sha256, utcnow, write_json
   11: from validate_pipeline import validate
   12: 
   13: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
   14: 
   15: 
   16: def run(input_file, timeout=300, demo_proof=None):
   17:     os.chdir(ROOT)
   18:     input_file = Path(input_file).resolve()
   19:     run_id = 'run-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
   20:     evidence = ROOT / 'evidence' / run_id
   21:     evidence.mkdir(parents=True)
   22:     commands = []
   23:     state = {'run_id': run_id, 'started_at_utc': utcnow(), 'status': 'NOT_RUN', 'input': str(input_file)}
   24:     env = os.environ.copy()
   25:     run_dir = ROOT / 'data' / 'runs' / run_id
   26:     log_dir = ROOT / 'logs' / run_id
   27:     env['RUN_DIR'], env['LOG_DIR'] = run_dir.as_posix(), log_dir.as_posix()
   28:     base = ['docker', 'compose', '-p', 'movie-' + run_id]
   29: 
   30:     def command(args, filename, check=True):
   31:         commands.append(args)
   32:         with (evidence / filename).open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
   33:             result = subprocess.run(args, env=env, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
   34:         if check and result.returncode:
   35:             raise RuntimeError(f'Command exited {result.returncode}; inspect {filename}')
   36:         return result
   37: 
   38:     started = False
   39:     try:
   40:         initial = validate(input_file, 'data/processed/movies.csv', report=evidence / 'input_validation.json')
   41:         if initial['status'] != 'INPUT_PASS':
   42:             raise ValueError('Input validation failed')
   43:         if initial['input']['total'] > 10000:
   44:             if demo_proof is None:
   45:                 raise ValueError('Main input requires --demo-proof evidence/<demo-run>/status.json')
   46:             proof_path = Path(demo_proof)
   47:             proof = json.loads(proof_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
   48:             demo_result = json.loads((proof_path.parent / 'reconciliation.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
   49:             if proof['status'] != 'PASS' or demo_result['status'] != 'PASS' or not 1 <= demo_result['input']['total'] <= 10000:
   50:                 raise ValueError('Demo proof is not a successful small E2E run')
   51:             if proof.get('config_sha256') != sha256('config/flume.conf'):
   52:                 raise ValueError('Config changed since demo; rerun demo')
   53:         if shutil.which('docker') is None:
   54:             state['status'] = 'BLOCKED_ENVIRONMENT'
   55:             raise RuntimeError('Docker executable not found. Install/start Docker Desktop with Linux containers.')
   56:         command(['docker', 'version'], 'docker-version.txt')
   57:         command(['docker', 'compose', 'version'], 'compose-version.txt')
   58:         for folder in ['spool', 'output', 'channel/checkpoint', 'channel/data']:
   59:             (run_dir / folder).mkdir(parents=True)
   60:         log_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
   61:         command(base + ['config'], 'compose-resolved.yaml')
   62:         command(base + ['build'], 'build.log')
   63:         started = True
   64:         command(base + ['up', '-d'], 'up.log')
   65:         command(base + ['images'], 'images.txt')
   66:         command(base + ['exec', '-T', 'flume', 'java', '-version'], 'java-version.txt')
   67:         command(base + ['exec', '-T', 'flume', '/opt/flume/bin/flume-ng', 'version'], 'flume-version.txt')
   68:         # Copy completely to an ignored temporary name, then atomically rename in SAME directory.
   69:         staged = run_dir / 'spool' / (uuid.uuid4().hex + '.jsonl')
   70:         temporary = staged.with_suffix('.tmp')
   71:         shutil.copyfile(input_file, temporary)
   72:         temporary.rename(staged)
   73:         state.update({'input_sha256': sha256(input_file), 'config_sha256': sha256('config/flume.conf'),
   74:                       'spooled_file': str(staged), 'output_dir': str(run_dir / 'output')})
   75:         deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
   76:         expected = initial['input']['total']
   77:         drained = False
   78:         while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   79:             completed = staged.with_name(staged.name + '.COMPLETED').exists()
   80:             count = 0
   81:             for path in (run_dir / 'output').glob('*'):
   82:                 if path.is_file():
   83:                     with path.open('rb') as f:
   84:                         count += sum(1 for line in f if line.endswith(b'\n'))
   85:             if completed and count >= expected:
   86:                 drained = True
   87:                 break
   88:             time.sleep(2)
   89:         command(base + ['stop'], 'stop.log')
   90:         # Reconcile only after sink is stopped and file buffers closed.
   91:         result = validate(input_file, 'data/processed/movies.csv', run_dir / 'output', evidence / 'reconciliation.json')
   92:         state['spool_completed'] = staged.with_name(staged.name + '.COMPLETED').exists()
   93:         state['status'] = 'PASS' if drained and state['spool_completed'] and result['status'] == 'PASS' else 'FAIL'
   94:         if not drained:
   95:             state['reason'] = 'Timeout waiting for ingestion/drain; reconciliation records actual output'
   96:         for label, paths in [('input', [input_file]), ('output', sorted((run_dir / 'output').glob('*')))]:
   97:             sample = []
   98:             for path in paths:
   99:                 if path.is_file():
  100:                     with path.open(encoding='utf-8') as f:
  101:                         for line in f:
  102:                             sample.append(line)
  103:                             if len(sample) == 5:
  104:                                 break
  105:                 if len(sample) == 5:
  106:                     break
  107:             (evidence / (label + '-sample.jsonl')).write_text(''.join(sample), encoding='utf-8')
  108:     except Exception as exc:
  109:         if state['status'] != 'BLOCKED_ENVIRONMENT':
  110:             state['status'] = 'FAIL'
  111:         state['reason'] = str(exc)
  112:     finally:
  113:         if started:
  114:             for args, filename in [(base + ['stop'], 'stop-final.log'),
  115:                                    (base + ['logs', '--no-color', '--timestamps'], 'flume-operational.log'),
  116:                                    (base + ['down'], 'down.log')]:
  117:                 try:
  118:                     command(args, filename, check=False)
  119:                 except Exception as exc:
  120:                     state['cleanup_error'] = str(exc)
  121:         state['finished_at_utc'] = utcnow()
  122:         write_json(evidence / 'commands.json', {'cwd': str(ROOT), 'environment': {'RUN_DIR': env['RUN_DIR'], 'LOG_DIR': env['LOG_DIR']}, 'commands': commands})
  123:         write_json(evidence / 'status.json', state)
  124:     print(json.dumps(state, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
  125:     return state
  126: 
  127: 
  128: if __name__ == '__main__':
  129:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  130:     p.add_argument('--input', default='data/generated/demo.jsonl')
  131:     p.add_argument('--timeout', type=int, default=300)
  132:     p.add_argument('--demo-proof')
  133:     a = p.parse_args()
  134:     result = run(a.input, a.timeout, a.demo_proof)
  135:     raise SystemExit(0 if result['status'] == 'PASS' else 1)

FILE: scripts/spark_smoke_test.py
    1: """Spark smoke test for Week 1 environment and schema verification.
    2: 
    3: Scope:
    4: - Validates SparkSession creation.
    5: - Prints Spark, Python, and Java environment info.
    6: - Reads movies.csv with explicit MOVIE_SCHEMA.
    7: - Reads Flume JSONL output with explicit USER_LOG_SCHEMA.
    8: - Verifies movieId join compatibility without running any aggregations or analysis.
    9: """
   10: import argparse
   11: import os
   12: import sys
   13: from pathlib import Path
   14: 
   15: # Add project root to sys.path so src.schemas can be imported
   16: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
   17: if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
   18:     sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
   19: 
   20: from src.schemas import MOVIE_SCHEMA, USER_LOG_SCHEMA
   21: 
   22: from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
   23: from pyspark.sql import functions as F
   24: 
   25: 
   26: def main():
   27:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Spark Week 1 Smoke Test")
   28:     parser.add_argument(
   29:         "--movies-path",
   30:         default=str(ROOT / "data" / "processed" / "movies.csv"),
   31:         help="Path to processed movies.csv",
   32:     )
   33:     parser.add_argument(
   34:         "--logs-path",
   35:         default=str(ROOT / "data" / "runs" / "run-f63c810e9d8d" / "output" / "*.jsonl"),
   36:         help="Glob pattern to Flume output jsonl files",
   37:     )

   39:

FILE: scripts/validate_pipeline.py
    1: """Streaming JSONL checks + on-disk SQLite reconciliation. Never simulates Flume."""
    2: import argparse
    3: import csv
    4: import hashlib
    5: import json
    6: import sqlite3
    7: import tempfile
    8: from collections import Counter
    9: from pathlib import Path
   10: from common import parse_time, sha256, utcnow, write_json
   11: 
   12: REQUIRED = {'eventId', 'userId', 'sessionId', 'movieId', 'action', 'timestamp', 'watchDuration',
   13:             'sequenceNumber', 'isSynthetic', 'simulatedMovieDuration', 'durationSource', 'runId'}
   14: 
   15: 
   16: def validate(input_path, movies, output_dir=None, report='evidence/validation.json'):
   17:     input_path = Path(input_path)
   18:     if not input_path.is_file():
   19:         raise FileNotFoundError(input_path)
   20:     with open(movies, encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:
   21:         mids = {int(r['movieId']) for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
   22:     stats = {'checked_at_utc': utcnow(), 'mode': 'input_only' if output_dir is None else 'pipeline_reconciliation',
   23:              'input_sha256': sha256(input_path), 'movies_sha256': sha256(movies), 'errors_sample': []}
   24:     with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='movie-validate-') as tmp:
   25:         db = sqlite3.connect(str(Path(tmp) / 'validation.sqlite'))
   26:         db.execute('PRAGMA cache_size=-16384')
   27:         db.execute('CREATE TABLE events (side TEXT, id TEXT, payload TEXT, sid TEXT, seq INT, PRIMARY KEY(side,id))')
   28:         db.execute('CREATE TABLE sessions (uid TEXT, start TEXT, end TEXT)')
   29:         counts = {s: Counter() for s in ['input', 'output']}
   30: 
   31:         def error(side, location, message):
   32:             counts[side]['invalid'] += 1
   33:             if len(stats['errors_sample']) < 20:
   34:                 stats['errors_sample'].append({'side': side, 'location': location, 'error': str(message)})
   35: 
   36:         def ingest(path, side):
   37:             with path.open('rb') as f:
   38:                 for number, line in enumerate(f, 1):
   39:                     counts[side]['total'] += 1
   40:                     try:
   41:                         if not line.endswith(b'\n'):
   42:                             raise ValueError('Missing newline / potentially truncated event')
   43:                         decoded = line.decode('utf-8', errors='strict')
   44:                         if len(decoded.rstrip('\r\n')) > 16384:
   45:                             raise ValueError('Event exceeds Flume LINE maxLineLength')
   46:                         e = json.loads(decoded)
   47:                         if not isinstance(e, dict) or not REQUIRED.issubset(e):
   48:                             raise ValueError('Missing required fields')
   49:                         for k in ['eventId', 'sessionId', 'userId', 'runId']:
   50:                             if not isinstance(e[k], str) or not e[k]:
   51:                                 raise ValueError('Invalid ' + k)
   52:                         for k in ['movieId', 'watchDuration', 'sequenceNumber', 'simulatedMovieDuration']:
   53:                             if type(e[k]) is not int or e[k] < 0:
   54:                                 raise ValueError('Invalid integer ' + k)
   55:                         if e['movieId'] not in mids:
   56:                             raise ValueError('Unknown movieId')
   57:                         if e['action'] not in ['view', 'play', 'pause', 'stop', 'complete']:
   58:                             raise ValueError('Unknown action')
   59:                         if e['action'] in ['view', 'play'] and e['watchDuration'] != 0:
   60:                             raise ValueError('view/play must have zero watchDuration')
   61:                         if not e['timestamp'].endswith('Z'):
   62:                             raise ValueError('Timestamp must use UTC Z')
   63:                         parse_time(e['timestamp'])
   64:                         if e['isSynthetic'] is not True or e['durationSource'] != 'synthetic' or e['simulatedMovieDuration'] <= 0:
   65:                             raise ValueError('Missing synthetic provenance')
   66:                         payload = json.dumps(e, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
   67:                         previous = db.execute('SELECT payload FROM events WHERE side=? AND id=?', (side, e['eventId'])).fetchone()
   68:                         if previous:
   69:                             counts[side]['duplicates'] += 1
   70:                             if previous[0] != payload:
   71:                                 counts[side]['conflicting_ids'] += 1
   72:                         else:
   73:                             db.execute('INSERT INTO events VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)', (side, e['eventId'], payload, e['sessionId'], e['sequenceNumber']))
   74:                             counts[side]['unique'] += 1
   75:                     except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError, UnicodeError) as exc:
   76:                         error(side, f'{path}:{number}', exc)
   77:                     if number % 10000 == 0:
   78:                         db.commit()
   79: 
   80:         ingest(input_path, 'input')
   81:         paths = [] if output_dir is None else sorted(p for p in Path(output_dir).glob('*') if p.is_file() and not p.name.startswith('.'))
   82:         for path in paths:
   83:             ingest(path, 'output')
   84:         db.commit()
   85:         db.execute('CREATE INDEX session_order ON events(side,sid,seq)')
   86:         # Validate input session semantics independent of physical order of the JSONL.
   87:         # Output is checked for identical canonical payload by eventId below.
   88:         prior_sid, group = None, []
   89:         session_errors, valid_sessions = 0, 0
   90:         def check_session(g):
   91:             nonlocal session_errors, valid_sessions
   92:             try:
   93:                 first = g[0]
   94:                 if first['action'] != 'view':
   95:                     raise ValueError('Session must begin with view')
   96:                 expected, playing, watched, previous_time = 0, False, 0, None
   97:                 previous_action = None
   98:                 for e in g:
   99:                     if any(e[k] != first[k] for k in ['movieId', 'userId', 'runId', 'simulatedMovieDuration']):
  100:                         raise ValueError('Session identity/runtime changed')
  101:                     if e['sequenceNumber'] != expected:
  102:                         raise ValueError('Missing/duplicate sequenceNumber')
  103:                     t = parse_time(e['timestamp'])
  104:                     if previous_time is not None and t <= previous_time:
  105:                         raise ValueError('Non-increasing session timestamp')
  106:                     action = e['action']
  107:                     if expected == 0:
  108:                         if action != 'view':
  109:                             raise ValueError('Missing initial view')
  110:                     elif action == 'play':
  111:                         if playing or previous_action not in ['view', 'pause']:
  112:                             raise ValueError('Invalid play transition')
  113:                         playing = True
  114:                     elif action in ['pause', 'stop', 'complete']:
  115:                         if not playing or e['watchDuration'] != int((t - previous_time).total_seconds()):
  116:                             raise ValueError('Invalid duration/transition from play')
  117:                         playing = False
  118:                         if action in ['stop', 'complete'] and expected != len(g) - 1:
  119:                             raise ValueError('Events after terminal action')
  120:                     else:
  121:                         raise ValueError('Repeated view')
  122:                     watched += e['watchDuration']
  123:                     expected += 1
  124:                     previous_time, previous_action = t, action
  125:                 if len(g) != 1 and g[-1]['action'] not in ['stop', 'complete']:
  126:                     raise ValueError('Unfinished session')
  127:                 if watched > first['simulatedMovieDuration'] or (g[-1]['action'] == 'complete' and watched != first['simulatedMovieDuration']):
  128:                     raise ValueError('Invalid completion/runtime total')
  129:                 if g[-1]['action'] == 'stop' and watched >= first['simulatedMovieDuration']:
  130:                     raise ValueError('stop must occur before complete')
  131:                 db.execute('INSERT INTO sessions VALUES(?,?,?)', (first['userId'], first['timestamp'], g[-1]['timestamp']))
  132:                 valid_sessions += 1
  133:             except ValueError as exc:
  134:                 session_errors += 1
  135:                 if len(stats['errors_sample']) < 20:
  136:                     stats['errors_sample'].append({'sessionId': g[0]['sessionId'], 'error': str(exc)})
  137:         for sid, payload in db.execute("SELECT sid,payload FROM events WHERE side='input' ORDER BY sid,seq"):
  138:             if prior_sid is not None and sid != prior_sid:
  139:                 check_session(group)
  140:                 group = []
  141:             group.append(json.loads(payload))
  142:             prior_sid = sid
  143:         if group:
  144:             check_session(group)
  145:         overlap, previous_uid, previous_end = 0, None, None
  146:         for uid, start, end in db.execute('SELECT uid,start,end FROM sessions ORDER BY uid,start'):
  147:             if uid == previous_uid and start <= previous_end:
  148:                 overlap += 1
  149:             previous_end = max(end, previous_end) if uid == previous_uid else end
  150:             previous_uid = uid
  151:         stats.update({'session_errors': session_errors, 'valid_sessions': valid_sessions, 'user_overlap_errors': overlap})
  152:         for side in counts:
  153:             stats[side] = {k: counts[side][k] for k in ['total', 'unique', 'duplicates', 'conflicting_ids', 'invalid']}
  154:         stats['output_files'] = [{'path': str(p), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha256(p)} for p in paths]
  155:         missing = db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM events i LEFT JOIN events o ON o.side='output' AND i.id=o.id WHERE i.side='input' AND o.id IS NULL").fetchone()[0]
  156:         extra = db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM events o LEFT JOIN events i ON i.side='input' AND i.id=o.id WHERE o.side='output' AND i.id IS NULL").fetchone()[0]
  157:         changed = db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM events i JOIN events o ON i.id=o.id AND o.side='output' WHERE i.side='input' AND i.payload<>o.payload").fetchone()[0]
  158:         stats.update({'missing_event_ids': missing if output_dir is not None else None,
  159:                       'unexpected_event_ids': extra if output_dir is not None else None,
  160:                       'changed_payloads': changed if output_dir is not None else None})
  161:         manifest_path = input_path.with_suffix(input_path.suffix + '.manifest.json')
  162:         manifest_errors = []
  163:         if manifest_path.exists():
  164:             manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
  165:             if manifest['events'] != stats['input']['total'] or manifest['sha256'] != stats['input_sha256']:
  166:                 manifest_errors.append('Input count/hash differs from manifest')
  167:             for (payload,) in db.execute("SELECT payload FROM events WHERE side='input'"):
  168:                 e = json.loads(payload)
  169:                 if not parse_time(manifest['start']) <= parse_time(e['timestamp']) < parse_time(manifest['end']):
  170:                     manifest_errors.append('Timestamp outside requested range')
  171:                     break
  172:         stats['manifest_errors'] = manifest_errors
  173:         failed = not stats['input']['total'] or session_errors or overlap or manifest_errors or any(sum(counts[s][k] for k in ['invalid', 'duplicates', 'conflicting_ids']) for s in counts)
  174:         if output_dir is not None:
  175:             failed = failed or not paths or missing or extra or changed
  176:         stats['status'] = 'FAIL' if failed else ('INPUT_PASS' if output_dir is None else 'PASS')
  177:         stats['semantics'] = 'Observed reconciliation only; not an exactly-once guarantee. Duplicates are reported as FAIL for review.'
  178:         db.close()
  179:     write_json(report, stats)
  180:     print(json.dumps(stats, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
  181:     return stats
  182: 
  183: 
  184: if __name__ == '__main__':
  185:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  186:     p.add_argument('--input', required=True)
  187:     p.add_argument('--movies', default='data/processed/movies.csv')
  188:     group = p.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
  189:     group.add_argument('--output-dir')
  190:     group.add_argument('--input-only', action='store_true')
  191:     p.add_argument('--report', default='evidence/validation.json')
  192:     a = p.parse_args()
  193:     result = validate(a.input, a.movies, a.output_dir, a.report)
  194:     raise SystemExit(1 if result['status'] == 'FAIL' else 0)

FILE: scripts/verify_archive.py
    1: """Accept Apache SHA512 formats; fail closed if checksum is absent/ambiguous."""
    2: import hashlib
    3: import re
    4: import sys
    5: from pathlib import Path
    6: 
    7: digests = re.findall(r'(?<![0-9a-fA-F])[0-9a-fA-F]{128}(?![0-9a-fA-F])', Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text())
    8: if len(digests) != 1:
    9:     raise SystemExit('Expected exactly one SHA512 digest from Apache')
   10: h = hashlib.sha512()
   11: with open(sys.argv[1], 'rb') as f:
   12:     for block in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
   13:         h.update(block)
   14: if h.hexdigest().lower() != digests[0].lower():
   15:     raise SystemExit('Archive SHA512 MISMATCH')
   16: print('Apache Flume archive SHA512 verified:', h.hexdigest())

   12: )

   17:     StructField("movieId", LongType(), nullable=False),
   18:     StructField("title", StringType(), nullable=False),

   38: ])

FILE: tests/test_project.py
    1: """Unit/integration tests use temporary fixtures, NOT Flume output evidence."""
    2: import contextlib
    3: import csv
    4: import io
    5: import json
    6: from pathlib import Path
    7: import sys
    8: import tempfile
    9: import unittest
   10: 
   11: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'scripts'))
   12: from generate_logs import generate
   13: from prepare_movies import prepare
   14: from validate_pipeline import validate
   15: 
   16: 
   17: class ProjectTests(unittest.TestCase):
   18:     def setUp(self):
   19:         self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
   20:         self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
   21:         self.movies = self.root / 'movies.csv'
   22:         self.movies.write_text('movieId,title,genres\n1,Phim Việt (2001),Drama\n2,No year,(no genres listed)\n3,Series (2000–2001),Comedy\n', encoding='utf-8')
   23:         self.output = self.root / 'events.jsonl'
   24:         self.sink = self.root / 'mock-sink'
   25:         self.sink.mkdir()
   26:         self.quiet = contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO())
   27:         self.quiet.__enter__()
   28: 
   29:     def tearDown(self):
   30:         self.quiet.__exit__(None, None, None)
   31:         self.temp.cleanup()
   32: 
   33:     def make(self, n=101):
   34:         generate(self.movies, self.output, events=n, users=100)
   35:         return self.output.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()
   36: 
   37:     def check(self, output_dir=None):
   38:         return validate(self.output, self.movies, output_dir, self.root / 'report.json')
   39: 
   40:     def test_normalization_preserves_missing_year(self):
   41:         normalized = self.root / 'normalized.csv'
   42:         result = prepare(self.movies, normalized, self.root / 'quality.json')
   43:         self.assertEqual(result['missing_or_unparsed_release_year'], 2)
   44:         with normalized.open(encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:
   45:             self.assertEqual(next(csv.DictReader(f))['releaseYear'], '2001')
   46: 
   47:     def test_duplicate_movies_rejected(self):
   48:         with self.movies.open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
   49:             f.write('1,Duplicate,Drama\n')
   50:         with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
   51:             prepare(self.movies, self.root / 'normalized.csv', self.root / 'quality.json')
   52: 
   53:     def test_invalid_utf8_rejected(self):
   54:         self.movies.write_bytes(b'movieId,title,genres\n1,\xff,Drama\n')
   55:         with self.assertRaises(UnicodeDecodeError):
   56:             prepare(self.movies, self.root / 'normalized.csv', self.root / 'quality.json')
   57: 
   58:     def test_exact_counts_boundaries_and_sessions(self):
   59:         for n in [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 17, 10000]:
   60:             path = self.root / f'events-{n}.jsonl'
   61:             generate(self.movies, path, events=n, users=1000)
   62:             result = validate(path, self.movies, report=self.root / f'report-{n}.json')
   63:             self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'INPUT_PASS')
   64:             self.assertEqual(result['input']['total'], n)
   65: 
   66:     def test_seed_reproducibility(self):
   67:         self.make()
   68:         other = self.root / 'repeat.jsonl'
   69:         generate(self.movies, other, events=101, users=100)
   70:         self.assertEqual(self.output.read_bytes(), other.read_bytes())
   71: 
   72:     def test_output_protection(self):
   73:         self.make()
   74:         with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):
   75:             generate(self.movies, self.output, events=101, users=100)
   76: 
   77:     def test_mock_reordered_output_matches(self):
   78:         rows = self.make()
   79:         (self.sink / 'part').write_text('\n'.join(reversed(rows)) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
   80:         self.assertEqual(self.check(self.sink)['status'], 'PASS')
   81: 
   82:     def test_missing_duplicate_invalid_and_mutated_detected(self):
   83:         rows = self.make()
   84:         changed = json.loads(rows[2])
   85:         changed['userId'] = 'tampered'
   86:         (self.sink / 'part').write_text('\n'.join([rows[0], rows[0], json.dumps(changed)] + rows[3:] + ['not json']) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
   87:         result = self.check(self.sink)
   88:         self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'FAIL')
   89:         self.assertEqual(result['missing_event_ids'], 1)
   90:         self.assertEqual(result['output']['duplicates'], 1)
   91:         self.assertEqual(result['output']['invalid'], 1)
   92:         self.assertEqual(result['changed_payloads'], 1)
   93: 
   94:     def test_empty_sink_never_passes(self):
   95:         self.make()
   96:         self.assertEqual(self.check(self.sink)['status'], 'FAIL')
   97: 
   98:     def test_unknown_movie_rejected(self):
   99:         rows = self.make()
  100:         event = json.loads(rows[0])
  101:         event['movieId'] = 99999999
  102:         rows[0] = json.dumps(event)
  103:         (self.sink / 'part').write_text('\n'.join(rows) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
  104:         self.assertEqual(self.check(self.sink)['output']['invalid'], 1)
  105: 
  106:     def test_duration_tampering_rejected(self):
  107:         rows = self.make()
  108:         for i, row in enumerate(rows):
  109:             event = json.loads(row)
  110:             if event['action'] in ['pause', 'stop', 'complete']:
  111:                 event['watchDuration'] += 1
  112:                 rows[i] = json.dumps(event)
  113:                 break
  114:         self.output.write_text('\n'.join(rows) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
  115:         self.assertGreater(self.check()['session_errors'], 0)
  116: 
  117:     def test_naive_timezone_rejected(self):
  118:         with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
  119:             generate(self.movies, self.output, events=10, start='2026-09-01T00:00:00')
  120: 
  121: 
  122: if __name__ == '__main__':
  123:     unittest.main(verbosity=2)
